for i in `cat samples`
do

(java -jar /home/david/ting/Trimmomatic-0.32/trimmomatic-0.32.jar PE R1.$i.fastq R2.$i.fastq R1.paired.$i.fastq R1.unpaired.$i.fastq R2.paired.$i.fastq R2.unpaired.$i.fastq SLIDINGWINDOW:5:20 MINLEN:200

usearch -fastq_mergepairs R1.paired.$i.fastq -reverse R2.paired.$i.fastq -fastqout merged.$i.fastq -fastq_truncqual 5 -fastq_maxdiffs 3 -fastq_minmergelen 200 -fastq_minovlen 20

#modified 16S forward
#16S for GTGyCAGCMGCCGCGGTAA
#modified 16S reverse
#16S rev CCGYCAATTYMTTTRAGTTT

#rev complement reverse
cutadapt -a AAACTYAAAKRAATTGRCGG$ merged.$i.fastq -o no.rev.merged.$i.fastq -e 0.2 --discard-untrimmed
#normal forward
cutadapt -g ^GTGYCAGCMGCCGCGGTAA no.rev.merged.$i.fastq -o no.prime.$i.fastq -e 0.2 --discard-untrimmed
seqtk seq -a no.prime.$i.fastq > no.prime.$i.fasta) &

done

cat no.prime*fasta /home/korriban/david/diatom_bloom_tag_analysis/stag_comm_4714_2_trimmed.fasta /home/korriban/david/diatom_bloom_tag_analysis/even_comm_4714_2_trimmed.fasta > in.silico.mocks.no.prime.merged.cat.all.esp.paired.fasta

nohup identify_chimeric_seqs.py -i in.silico.mocks.no.prime.merged.cat.all.esp.paired.fasta -m usearch61 -r /home/korriban/david/diatom_bloom_tag_analysis/gold.fa --non_chimeras_retention intersection -o esp.list_of_chimeras_all.fasta &


filter_fasta.py -f in.silico.mocks.no.prime.merged.cat.all.esp.paired.fasta -o no.chimeras.in.silico.mocks.no.prime.merged.cat.all.esp.paired.fasta -n -s esp.list_of_chimeras_all.fasta/chimeras.txt


#####################
#####merged reads MED
#####################

cut -f1 no.chimeras.in.silico.mocks.no.prime.merged.cat.all.esp.paired.fasta -d ' '  > cut.no.chimeras.in.silico.mocks.no.prime.merged.cat.all.esp.paired.fasta
o-pad-with-gaps cut.no.chimeras.in.silico.mocks.no.prime.merged.cat.all.esp.paired.fasta -o pad.no.chimeras.in.silico.mocks.no.prime.merged.cat.all.esp.paired.fasta

nohup decompose pad.no.chimeras.in.silico.mocks.no.prime.merged.cat.all.esp.paired.fasta -o med.out.5 --skip-gen-figures &

cd med.out.5

sed 's/-//g' NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta > no.dash.NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta

#16S Silva taxonomy assignment
nohup assign_taxonomy.py -i no.dash.NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta -r /home/korriban/david/diatom_bloom_tag_analysis/Silva_111_post/rep_set/99_Silva_111_rep_set.fasta -t /home/korriban/david/diatom_bloom_tag_analysis/Silva_111_post/taxonomy/99_Silva_111_taxa_map_RDP_6_levels.txt -o silva_NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta &
#location
/korriban/david/esp.tag/esp.tag.3/esp.seqs.all/med.out.5/silva_NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta/no.dash.NODE-REPRESENTATIVES_tax_assignments.txt

cut -f2 /home/korriban/david/esp.tag/esp.tag.3/esp.seqs.all/med.out.5/silva_NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta/no.dash.NODE-REPRESENTATIVES_tax_assignments.txt | sed 's/; __/ /g' > delimited.silva.tax
cut -f1 /home/korriban/david/esp.tag/esp.tag.3/esp.seqs.all/med.out.5/silva_NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta/no.dash.NODE-REPRESENTATIVES_tax_assignments.txt | cut -f1 -d "|" > silva.tax.id.number
paste silva.tax.id.number delimited.silva.tax -d ' ' > silva.id.w.tax

#16S greengenes taxonomy assignment
nohup assign_taxonomy.py -i no.dash.NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta -r /home/korriban/david/diatom_bloom_tag_analysis/gg_13_8_otus/rep_set/99_otus.fasta -t /home/korriban/david/diatom_bloom_tag_analysis/gg_13_8_otus/taxonomy/99_otu_taxonomy.txt -o gg_NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta &
/korriban/david/esp.tag/esp.tag.3/esp.seqs.all/med.out.5/gg_NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta/no.dash.NODE-REPRESENTATIVES_tax_assignments.txt


cut -f2 /home/korriban/david/esp.tag/esp.tag.3/esp.seqs.all/med.out.5/gg_NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta/no.dash.NODE-REPRESENTATIVES_tax_assignments.txt | sed 's/; __/ /g' > delimited.gg.tax
cut -f1 /home/korriban/david/esp.tag/esp.tag.3/esp.seqs.all/med.out.5/gg_NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta/no.dash.NODE-REPRESENTATIVES_tax_assignments.txt | cut -f1 -d "|" > gg.tax.id.number
paste gg.tax.id.number delimited.gg.tax -d ' ' > gg.id.w.tax




#cat /home/db/sequence.gi.txt extra.removal > gi.remove

#16S classification with NCBI
nohup blastn -db /korriban/david/diatom_bloom_tag_analysis/nt -negative_gilist /korriban/david/esp.tag/esp.tag.3/esp.seqs.all/gi.remove -num_threads 120 -query no.dash.NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta -out top1.ncbi.seqs.fa -outfmt "6 qseqid sseqid pident length mismatch gapopen qstart qend sstart send evalue bitscore sseqid sallseqid sgi sacc staxids sscinames scomnames stitle" -max_target_seqs 1 &
#location
/home/korriban/david/esp.tag/esp.tag.3/esp.seqs.all/med.out.5/top1.ncbi.seqs.fa


#16S classification with PhytoRef
nohup blastn -db /home/korriban/david/diatom_bloom_tag_analysis/phytoref.db.name -num_threads 120 -query no.dash.NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta -out top1.phytoref.seqs.fa -outfmt "6 qseqid sseqid pident length mismatch gapopen qstart qend sstart send evalue bitscore sseqid sallseqid sgi sacc staxids sscinames scomnames stitle" -max_target_seqs 1 &
#location
/home/korriban/david/esp.tag/esp.tag.3/esp.seqs.all/med.out.5/top1.phytoref.seqs.fa

#16S classification with PhytoRef

blastn -db /home/korriban/david/diatom_bloom_tag_analysis/phytoref.db -num_threads 120 -query /home/korriban/david/esp.tag/esp.tag.3/esp.seqs.all/med.out.5/no.dash.NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta -out phytoref.full.taxonomy.no.dash.NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta -outfmt 6 -max_target_seqs 1 &

cut -f2 phytoref.full.taxonomy.no.dash.NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta | sed 's/|/ /g' > delimited.phytoref.tax
cut -f1 -d "|" phytoref.full.taxonomy.no.dash.NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta > phytoref.tax.id.number
paste phytoref.tax.id.number delimited.phytoref.tax -d ' ' > phytoref.id.w.tax


#ncbi classifcation with top 3
nohup blastn -db /korriban/david/diatom_bloom_tag_analysis/nt -negative_gilist /korriban/david/esp.tag/esp.tag.3/esp.seqs.all/gi.remove -num_threads 120 -query /home/korriban/david/esp.tag/esp.tag.3/esp.seqs.all/med.out.5/no.dash.NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta -out top3.ncbi.seqs.fa -outfmt "6 qseqid sseqid pident length mismatch gapopen qstart qend sstart send evalue bitscore sseqid sallseqid sgi sacc staxids sscinames scomnames stitle" -max_target_seqs 3 &








cd med.out.5

sort gg_NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta/*.txt | sed 's/\t/|/g' | cut -f1,3 -d "|" | sed 's/|/\t/g' > sort.gg

cd med.out.5

R
#transpose meren's backwards formatting
a <- t(read.table("MATRIX-COUNT.txt",header=T, row.names=1))
###write.table(a,"tr.matrix",quote=F,sep="\t",col.names=NA)
quit('no')


sed -s 's/X//g' tr.matrix > no.x.tr.matrix
sort no.x.tr.matrix > sort.no.x.tr.matrix

#combine denovo names with gg tax

cd /home/korriban/david/esp.tag/esp.tag.3/esp.seqs.all/med.out.5/

join -1 1 -2 1 sort.no.x.tr.matrix sort.gg > table.w.gg.tax



#make sepearate tables for chloroplasts and non chloroplasts
grep "00000" table.w.gg.tax | rev | cut -f7- -d ';' | cut -f2- -d ' ' | rev  > table.w.gg.tax.2
awk 'NF' table.w.gg.tax.2 > table.w.gg.tax
grep "Chloroplast" table.w.gg.tax  | rev | cut -f7- -d ';' | cut -f2- -d ' ' | rev  > table.w.gg.tax.chl
grep -v "Chloroplast" table.w.gg.tax | rev | cut -f5- -d ';' | cut -f2- -d ' ' | rev | cut -f1-228 -d ' ' > table.w.gg.tax.wochl
grep "Cyanobacteria" table.w.gg.tax | rev | cut -f5- -d ';' | cut -f2- -d ' ' | rev | cut -f1-228 -d ' ' > table.w.gg.tax.phyto
grep -v "Cyanobacteria" table.w.gg.tax | rev | cut -f5- -d ';' | cut -f2- -d ' ' | rev | cut -f1-228 -d ' ' > table.w.gg.tax.nonphyto

#get just the dates
head -1 no.x.tr.matrix > dates

#combine the dates with the chlorophyll matrices
cat dates table.w.gg.tax | sed -e 's/^/X/' > table.w.gg.tax.txt
cat dates table.w.gg.tax.chl | sed -e 's/^/X/' > table.w.gg.tax.chl.txt
cat dates table.w.gg.tax.wochl | sed -e 's/^/X/'> table.w.gg.tax.wochl.txt
cat dates table.w.gg.tax.phyto | sed -e 's/^/X/'> table.w.gg.tax.phyto.txt
cat dates table.w.gg.tax.nonphyto | sed -e 's/^/X/'> table.w.gg.tax.nonphyto.txt

#make Silva Tag for Bact
cut -f2 silva_NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta/*txt | sed 's/; __g//g' | sed 's/; __f//g' | sed 's/; __o;//g' | sed 's/; __c;//g' | sed 's/; __/;/g' | sed 's/;o//g' | sed 's/ __o//g' | sed 's/;marine_group//g' | sed 's/(/_/g' | sed 's/)/_/g' | sed 's/Surface_/SAR11_Surface_/g' | rev | cut -f1 -d ";" | rev | sed 's/:/_/g' > silva.tag

#make NCBI tag for bact and chl
cut -f19 top1.ncbi.seqs.fa | cut -f1,2,3,4 -d " " | sed 's/,//g' | sed 's/ chloroplast//g' | sed 's/ culture-collection//g' | sed 's/ isolate//g' | sed 's/ chromosome//g' | sed 's/ strain//g' | sed 's/ plastid//g' | sed 's/clone 124/clone_124/g' | cut -f1,2,3 -d " " | sed 's/ complete//g' | sed 's/16S//g' | sed 's/ Pg-G(A)//g' | sed 's/ gene//g' |sed 's/:/_/g' | sed 's/\//_/g' > ncbi.tag

#make phytoref tag for bact and chl
cut -f19 top1.phytoref.seqs.fa | rev | cut -f1 -d "|" | rev | sed 's/ /_/g' > phytoref.tag

#get denovo prok names
cut -f1 silva_NODE-REPRESENTATIVES.fasta/*txt > denovo.silva.names
cut -f1 top1.ncbi.seqs.fa > denovo.ncbi.names
cut -f1 top1.phytoref.seqs.fa > denovo.phytoref.names

paste denovo.ncbi.names ncbi.tag | sort -k1,1 | sed 's/ /_/g' > denovo.ncbi.tag
paste denovo.silva.names silva.tag | sort -k1,1 | sed 's/ /_/g' > denovo.silva.tag
paste denovo.phytoref.names phytoref.tag | sort -k1,1 > denovo.phytoref.tag

join -1 1 -2 1 denovo.ncbi.tag denovo.silva.tag | sed 's/ /|/g' | cut -f1,3,4 -d '|' | sed 's/|/ /g' > join.denovo.ncbi.silva
join -1 1 -2 1 denovo.ncbi.tag denovo.phytoref.tag | sed 's/ /|/g' | cut -f1,3,4 -d '|' | sed 's/|/ /g' > join.denovo.ncbi.phytoref

cut -f2,3 -d " " join.denovo.ncbi.silva | sed 's/ /|/g' > concat.ncbi.silva.tags
cut -f2,3 -d " " join.denovo.ncbi.phytoref | sed 's/ /|/g' > concat.ncbi.phytoref.tags
cut -f1 -d ' ' join.denovo.ncbi.silva > denovo.names.prok
cut -f1 -d ' ' join.denovo.ncbi.phytoref > denovo.names.chl

#two columns, denovo name and abbreviated NCBI chloroplast name
paste denovo.names.prok concat.ncbi.silva.tags | sed -e 's/^/X/' | uniq > denovo.prok.silva.tag
paste denovo.names.chl concat.ncbi.phytoref.tags | sed -e 's/^/X/' | uniq > denovo.prok.phytoref.tag

cut -f1 table.w.gg.tax.wochl.txt -d ' ' | tail -n +2 > prok.denovo.ids
cut -f1 table.w.gg.tax.chl.txt -d ' ' | tail -n +2 > chl.denovo.ids

grep -f prok.denovo.ids denovo.prok.silva.tag > prok.only.denovo.prok.silva.tag
grep -f chl.denovo.ids denovo.prok.phytoref.tag > chl.only.denovo.prok.phytoref.tag

(java -jar /home/david/ting/Trimmomatic-0.32/trimmomatic-0.32.jar SE R1.paired.$i.fastq trim.R1.paired.$i.fastq MINLEN:290
java -jar /home/david/ting/Trimmomatic-0.32/trimmomatic-0.32.jar SE R2.paired.$i.fastq trim.R2.paired.$i.fastq MINLEN:225

filter_fasta.py -f trim.R2.paired.$i.fastq -o matches_R1.$i.fastq -s trim.R1.paired.$i.fastq.seqlist

filter_fasta.py -f trim.R1.paired.$i.fastq -o matches_R2.$i.fastq -s matches_R1.$i.fastq.seqlist

perl /home/korriban/david/metagamppool/prinseq-lite.pl -fastq matches_R1.$i.fastq -trim_to_len 225 -out_good matches.R2.$i.fastq
pear -f matches.R1.$i.fastq.fastq -r matches.R2.$i.fastq.fastq -o pear.test.$i
convert_fastaqual_fastq.py -f pear.test.$i.unassembled.forward.fastq -o pear.unmerged.r1.$i.fasta -c fastq_to_fastaqual
convert_fastaqual_fastq.py -f pear.test.$i.unassembled.reverse.fastq -o pear.unmerged.r2.$i.fasta -c fastq_to_fastaqual

sed -e 's/\(^>.*$\)/#\1#/' pear.unmerged.r1.$i.fasta/pear*.fna | tr -d "\r" | tr -d "\n" | sed -e 's/$/#/' | tr "#" "\n" | sed -e '/^$/d' > linear.unmerged.r1.$i.fna
sed -e 's/\(^>.*$\)/#\1#/' pear.unmerged.r2.$i.fasta/pear*.fna | tr -d "\r" | tr -d "\n" | sed -e 's/$/#/' | tr "#" "\n" | sed -e '/^$/d' > linear.unmerged.r2.$i.fna

sed -e 's/^/-/' linear.unmerged.r2.$i.fna |  sed -e 's/->/>/' > Ns.rc.linear.unmerged.r2.$i.fna
cut -f1 -d '>' Ns.rc.linear.unmerged.r2.$i.fna > Ns.rc.linear.unmerged.r2.$i.fna.no.header
paste linear.unmerged.r1.$i.fna Ns.rc.linear.unmerged.r2.$i.fna.no.header -d '' > combined.seq.$i.fa) &
done

R

#################################
#processing of environmental data
###############################


#import sample times
start.stop <- read.table("/home/korriban/david/esp.tag/sample.start.stop",sep="\t",header=T,stringsAsFactors=F,na.strings = "na")

#import esp environmental data
env <- read.table("/home/korriban/david/esp.tag/Catalina_CTD_to_USC.txt",sep="\t",header=T,stringsAsFactors=F,na.strings = "na")

#import tide data
#tide data downloaded from https://tidesandcurrents.noaa.gov/waterlevels.html?id=9410660&units=metric&bdate=20140417&edate=20140517&timezone=GMT&datum=MLLW&interval=6&action=
tide <- read.table("/home/korriban/david/esp.tag/tide.data.txt",sep="\t",header=T,stringsAsFactors=F)

#format times
env$date.time <- as.POSIXct(env$date.time, format="%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
start.stop$start <- as.POSIXct(start.stop$start, format="%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
start.stop$end <- as.POSIXct(start.stop$end, format="%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
tide$date.time <- as.POSIXct(tide$date.time, format="%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")


#bin esp environmental data
event.indices = sapply(env$date.time, function(d) which(d >= start.stop$start & d <= start.stop$end))
event.indices.2 = sapply(event.indices, function(i) ifelse(is.null(i), NA, i))

#bin tide data
tide.indices = sapply(tide$date.time, function(d) which(d >= start.stop$start & d <= start.stop$end))
tide.indices.2 = sapply(tide.indices, function(i) ifelse(is.null(i), NA, i))

#add sample points to the environmental and tide data
env.w.timept <- cbind(env,event.indices.2)
tide.w.timept <- cbind(tide,tide.indices.2)

#average the environmental and tide points for points during sampling events
aggdata <-aggregate(env.w.timept, by=list(event.indices.2), FUN=mean, na.rm=TRUE)
tide.data.agg <- aggregate(tide.w.timept, by=list(tide.indices.2), FUN=mean, na.rm=TRUE)

#combine the environmental and tide data
agg.data.w.tide <- cbind(aggdata,tide.data.agg[,3])
colnames(agg.data.w.tide)[12] <- "tide"
colnames(agg.data.w.tide)[11] <- "sample.number"

#colnames(aggdata)[11] <- "sample.number"

############################
#12 hour binning, same analysis as above
start.stop.12hr <- read.table("/home/korriban/david/esp.tag/12.hr",sep="\t",header=T,stringsAsFactors=F,na.strings = "na")
env.12hr <- read.table("/home/korriban/david/esp.tag/Catalina_CTD_to_USC.txt",sep="\t",header=T,stringsAsFactors=F,na.strings = "na")
tide.12hr <- read.table("/home/korriban/david/esp.tag/tide.data.txt",sep="\t",header=T,stringsAsFactors=F)
tide.12hr$date.time <- as.POSIXct(tide.12hr$date.time, format="%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
env.12hr$date.time <- as.POSIXct(env.12hr$date.time, format="%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
start.stop.12hr$start <- as.POSIXct(start.stop.12hr$start, format="%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
start.stop.12hr$end <- as.POSIXct(start.stop.12hr$end, format="%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
event.indices.12hr = sapply(env.12hr$date.time, function(d) which(d >= start.stop.12hr$start & d <= start.stop.12hr$end))
event.indices.2.12hr = sapply(event.indices.12hr, function(i) ifelse(is.null(i), NA, i))
tide.indices.12hr = sapply(tide.12hr$date.time, function(d) which(d >= start.stop.12hr$start & d <= start.stop.12hr$end))
tide.indices.2.12hr = sapply(tide.indices.12hr, function(i) ifelse(is.null(i), NA, i))
env.w.timept.12hr <- cbind(env.12hr,event.indices.2.12hr)
tide.w.timept.12hr <- cbind(tide.12hr,tide.indices.2.12hr)
aggdata.12hr <-aggregate(env.w.timept.12hr, by=list(event.indices.2.12hr), FUN=mean, na.rm=TRUE)
tide.data.agg.12hr <- aggregate(tide.w.timept.12hr, by=list(tide.indices.2.12hr), FUN=mean, na.rm=TRUE)
agg.data.w.tide.12hr <- cbind(aggdata.12hr,tide.data.agg.12hr[,3])
colnames(agg.data.w.tide.12hr)[12] <- "tide"
colnames(agg.data.w.tide.12hr)[11] <- "sample.number"

euk.18S.heterotrophs.w.metazoa <- as.data.frame(read.table("w.metazoan.heterotrophs.denovo.euk.pr2.ncbi.tag.txt", header=T, row.names=1))
euk.18S.w.metazoa <- as.data.frame(read.table("w.metazoan.denovo.euk.pr2.ncbi.tag.txt", header=T, row.names=1))
all.16S <- as.data.frame(read.table("med.out.5/table.w.gg.tax.txt", header=T, row.names=1))
phyto.16S <- as.data.frame(read.table("med.out.5/table.w.gg.tax.phyto.txt", header=T, row.names=1))
nonphyto.16S <- as.data.frame(read.table("med.out.5/table.w.gg.tax.nonphyto.txt", header=T, row.names=1))

t.euk.18S.w.metazoa <- as.data.frame(t(euk.18S.w.metazoa))
t.all.16S <- as.data.frame(t(all.16S))
prok.euk <-  merge(t.all.16S,t.euk.18S.w.metazoa,by="row.names",all.x=T,all.y=T)

rownames(prok.euk) <- prok.euk[,1]
t.prok.euk <- as.data.frame(t(prok.euk[,-1]))



#remove low samples
prok.16S.1 <- prok.16S[colSums(prok.16S) > 100]
chl.16S.1 <- chl.16S[colSums(chl.16S) > 100]
all.16S.1 <- all.16S[colSums(all.16S) > 100]
phyto.16S.1 <- phyto.16S[colSums(phyto.16S) > 100]
euk.18S.1 <- euk.18S[colSums(euk.18S) > 100]
euk.no.dino.18S.1 <- euk.18S.phototroph.no.dino[colSums(euk.18S.phototroph.no.dino) > 100]
euk.w.dino.18S.1 <- euk.18S.phototroph.w.dino[colSums(euk.18S.phototroph.w.dino) > 100]
euk.no.dino.18S.1 <- euk.18S.phototroph.no.dino[colSums(euk.18S.phototroph.no.dino) > 100]
euk.18S.heterotrophs.w.metazoa.1 <- euk.18S.heterotrophs.w.metazoa[colSums(euk.18S.heterotrophs.w.metazoa) > 100]
euk.18S.heterotrophs.1 <- euk.18S.heterotrophs[colSums(euk.18S.heterotrophs) > 100]
euk.18S.w.metazoa.1 <- euk.18S.w.metazoa[colSums(euk.18S.w.metazoa) > 100]
t.prok.euk.1 <- t.prok.euk[colSums(t.prok.euk,na.rm=T) > 100]
nonphyto.16S.1 <- nonphyto.16S[colSums(nonphyto.16S) > 100]


#normalize
prok.16S.2 <- as.data.frame(t(t(prok.16S.1)/rowSums(t(prok.16S.1))))
chl.16S.2 <- as.data.frame(t(t(chl.16S.1)/rowSums(t(chl.16S.1))))
all.16S.2 <- as.data.frame(t(t(all.16S.1)/rowSums(t(all.16S.1))))
phyto.16S.2 <- as.data.frame(t(t(phyto.16S.1)/rowSums(t(phyto.16S.1))))
euk.18S.2 <- as.data.frame(t(t(euk.18S.1)/rowSums(t(euk.18S.1))))
euk.no.dino.18S.2 <- as.data.frame(t(t(euk.no.dino.18S.1)/rowSums(t(euk.no.dino.18S.1))))
euk.w.dino.18S.2 <- as.data.frame(t(t(euk.w.dino.18S.1)/rowSums(t(euk.w.dino.18S.1))))
euk.18S.heterotrophs.w.metazoa.2 <- as.data.frame(t(t(euk.18S.heterotrophs.w.metazoa.1)/rowSums(t(euk.18S.heterotrophs.w.metazoa.1))))
euk.18S.heterotrophs.2 <- as.data.frame(t(t(euk.18S.heterotrophs.1)/rowSums(t(euk.18S.heterotrophs.1))))
t.prok.euk.2  <- as.data.frame(t(t(t.prok.euk.1)/rowSums(t(t.prok.euk.1))))
euk.18S.w.metazoa.2  <- as.data.frame(t(t(euk.18S.w.metazoa.1)/rowSums(t(euk.18S.w.metazoa.1))))

nonphyto.16S.2 <- as.data.frame(t(t(nonphyto.16S.1)/rowSums(t(nonphyto.16S.1))))

#count the number of sequences per sample for the different datasets

mean(colSums(euk.18S.1))
sd(colSums(euk.18S.1))

mean(colSums(nonphyto.16S.1[1:202]))
sd(colSums(nonphyto.16S.1[1:202]))

mean(colSums(phyto.16S.1[1:204]))
sd(colSums(phyto.16S.1[1:204]))

#add names to data frames
merged.prok <- merge(short.name,prok.16S.2,by="row.names")
merged.chl <- merge(chl.name,chl.16S.2,by="row.names")
merged.euk <- merge(euk.name,euk.18S.w.metazoa.2,by="row.names")

#replace rownames
rownames(merged.prok) <- merged.prok$"Row.names"
rownames(merged.chl) <- merged.chl$"Row.names"
rownames(merged.euk) <- merged.euk$"Row.names"

#add mean column
merged.prok$mean <- rowMeans(merged.prok[,c(3:208)])
merged.chl$mean <- rowMeans(merged.chl[,c(3:199)])
merged.euk$mean <- rowMeans(merged.euk[,c(3:97)])

#split by taxonomy
merged.split.prok <- split(merged.prok,merged.prok["V2"])
merged.split.chl <- split(merged.chl,merged.chl["V2"])
merged.split.euk <- split(merged.euk,merged.euk["V2"])

#filter for those that don't have more than one row i think
merged.split.prok.2 <- Filter(function(x) dim(x)[1] > 0, merged.split.prok)
merged.split.chl.2 <- Filter(function(x) dim(x)[1] > 0, merged.split.chl)
merged.split.euk.2 <- Filter(function(x) dim(x)[1] > 0, merged.split.euk)

#sort within taxonomy by mean abundance
merged.split.prok.3 <- lapply(merged.split.prok.2, function(x) x[order(-x$mean),] )
merged.split.chl.3 <- lapply(merged.split.chl.2, function(x) x[order(-x$mean),] )
merged.split.euk.3 <- lapply(merged.split.euk.2, function(x) x[order(-x$mean),] )

#make list of just the row orders to concatenate
merged.split.prok.4 <- lapply(merged.split.prok.3, function(x) 1:nrow(x))
merged.split.chl.4 <- lapply(merged.split.chl.3, function(x) 1:nrow(x))
merged.split.euk.4 <- lapply(merged.split.euk.3, function(x) 1:nrow(x))

#add the merged.split.3 to merged.split.2
merged.split.prok.5 <- mapply(cbind,merged.split.prok.3,merged.split.prok.4,SIMPLIFY=F)
merged.split.chl.5 <- mapply(cbind,merged.split.chl.3,merged.split.chl.4,SIMPLIFY=F)
merged.split.euk.5 <- mapply(cbind,merged.split.euk.3,merged.split.euk.4,SIMPLIFY=F)

#concatenate numbers to taxa names, number is last column
concate.names.prok <- lapply(merged.split.prok.5, function(x) paste(x$V2,x[,216],sep="_"))
concate.names.chl <- lapply(merged.split.chl.5, function(x) paste(x$V2,x[,201],sep="_"))
concate.names.euk <- lapply(merged.split.euk.5, function(x) paste(x$V2,x[,99],sep="_"))

merged.split.prok.6 <- mapply(cbind,merged.split.prok.5,concate.names.prok,SIMPLIFY=F)
merged.split.chl.6 <- mapply(cbind,merged.split.chl.5,concate.names.chl,SIMPLIFY=F)
merged.split.euk.6 <- mapply(cbind,merged.split.euk.5,concate.names.euk,SIMPLIFY=F)

#select again for only samples, plus the name (the last column)
merged.split.prok.7 <- lapply(merged.split.prok.6, function(x) x[,c(3:208,217)])
merged.split.chl.7 <- lapply(merged.split.chl.6, function(x) x[,c(3:199,202)])
merged.split.euk.7 <- lapply(merged.split.euk.6, function(x) x[,c(3:97,100)])



#rename colnames, can get both names, here
replacement.names.prok <- c(colnames(merged.split.prok.7$`AEGEAN-169_marine_group`[1:paste(length(merged.split.prok.7$`AEGEAN-169_marine_group`)-1)]),"tax.and.number")
replacement.names.chl <- c(colnames(merged.split.chl.7$`Aureococcus_anophagefferens`[1:paste(length(merged.split.chl.7$`Aureococcus_anophagefferens`)-1)]),"tax.and.number")
replacement.names.euk <- c(colnames(merged.split.euk.7$"Euk_Acantharea_sp."[1:paste(length(merged.split.euk.7$"Euk_Acantharea_sp.")-1)]),"tax.and.number")

#rename colnames with replacement names
merged.split.prok.8 <- lapply(merged.split.prok.7,setNames,replacement.names.prok)
merged.split.chl.8 <- lapply(merged.split.chl.7,setNames,replacement.names.chl)
merged.split.euk.8 <- lapply(merged.split.euk.7,setNames,replacement.names.euk)

#convert to dataframe
df.merged.split.prok.8 <- do.call(rbind,merged.split.prok.8)
df.merged.split.chl.8 <- do.call(rbind,merged.split.chl.8)
df.merged.split.euk.8 <- do.call(rbind,merged.split.euk.8)

#rename rownames
rownames(df.merged.split.prok.8) <- df.merged.split.prok.8$"tax.and.number"
rownames(df.merged.split.chl.8) <- df.merged.split.chl.8$"tax.and.number"
rownames(df.merged.split.euk.8) <- df.merged.split.euk.8$"tax.and.number"

#remove tax name col
df.merged.split.prok.9 <- df.merged.split.prok.8[,-c(207)]
df.merged.split.chl.9 <- df.merged.split.chl.8[,-c(198)]
df.merged.split.euk.9 <- df.merged.split.euk.8[,-c(96)]


#select the otu numbers

x.chl <- as.vector(unlist(lapply(merged.split.chl.6,rownames)))
x.euk <- as.vector(unlist(lapply(merged.split.euk.6,rownames)))

y.chl <- as.vector(unlist(lapply(merged.split.chl.6,function(x) x[[202]])))
y.euk <- as.vector(unlist(lapply(merged.split.euk.6,function(x) x[[100]])))

x.y.euk <- as.data.frame(cbind(x.euk,y.euk))


colnames(x.y.euk) <- c("xprok.xchl","yprok.ychl")




xprok.xchl <- c(x.prok,x.chl)
yprok.ychl <- c(y.prok,y.chl)

xprok.xchl.yprok.ychl <- as.data.frame(cbind(xprok.xchl,yprok.ychl))

xprok.xchl.yprok.ychl.xy.euk <- as.data.frame(rbind(xprok.xchl.yprok.ychl,x.y.euk))

merged.all.16S <- merge(xprok.xchl.yprok.ychl,all.16S.2,by.x="xprok.xchl",by.y="row.names")
euk.no.dino.18S.2.name.merge <- merge(euk.no.dino.18S.2,x.y.euk,by.x="row.names",by.y="xprok.xchl")
euk.w.dino.18S.2.name.merge <- merge(euk.w.dino.18S.2,x.y.euk,by.x="row.names",by.y="xprok.xchl")
euk.18S.2.name.merge <- merge(euk.18S.2,x.y.euk,by.x="row.names",by.y="xprok.xchl")

t.prok.euk.2.name.merge <- merge(t.prok.euk.2,xprok.xchl.yprok.ychl.xy.euk,by.x="row.names",by.y="xprok.xchl")

rownames(merged.all.16S) <- merged.all.16S$yprok.ychl
rownames(euk.18S.2.name.merge) <- euk.18S.2.name.merge$yprok.ychl
rownames(euk.w.dino.18S.2.name.merge) <- euk.w.dino.18S.2.name.merge$yprok.ychl
rownames(euk.no.dino.18S.2.name.merge) <- euk.no.dino.18S.2.name.merge$yprok.ychl
rownames(t.prok.euk.2.name.merge) <- t.prok.euk.2.name.merge$yprok.ychl


merged.all.16S.2 <- merged.all.16S[,-1]
euk.18S.2.name.merge.2 <- euk.18S.2.name.merge[,-c(1,101)]
euk.w.dino.18S.2.name.merge.2 <- euk.w.dino.18S.2.name.merge[,-c(1,82)]
euk.no.dino.18S.2.name.merge.2 <- euk.no.dino.18S.2.name.merge[,-c(1,82)]

merged.prok.euk.2 <- t.prok.euk.2.name.merge[,-c(1,217)]

#make attribute table
names <- rownames(at.least.75percent.lsa.rna.dna.prok.euk.chl.no.ratio.date.sort.plus.env.plusoccurr)
means <- rowMeans(at.least.75percent.lsa.rna.dna.prok.euk.chl.no.ratio.date.sort.plus.env.plusoccurr,na.rm=T)
data.type <- sub("_.*$","",rownames(at.least.75percent.lsa.rna.dna.prok.euk.chl.no.ratio.date.sort.plus.env.plusoccurr))
name.2 <- gsub("^.*?_","",rownames(at.least.75percent.lsa.rna.dna.prok.euk.chl.no.ratio.date.sort.plus.env.plusoccurr))

x.type.att <- sub("_.*$","",rownames(at.least.75percent.lsa.rna.dna.prok.euk.chl.no.ratio.date.sort.plus.env.plusoccurr))
x.type.2.att <- sub("\\..*$","",rownames(at.least.75percent.lsa.rna.dna.prok.euk.chl.no.ratio.date.sort.plus.env.plusoccurr))
general.name.x.att <- gsub("^.*?_","",rownames(at.least.75percent.lsa.rna.dna.prok.euk.chl.no.ratio.date.sort.plus.env.plusoccurr))

y.type <- sub("_.*$","",lsa.out.3$Y )

y.type.2 <- sub("\\..*$","",lsa.out.3$Y )

general.name.y <- gsub("^.*?_","",lsa.out.3$Y)
x.y.type <- paste(x.type,y.type,sep="_")
x.y.type.2 <- paste(x.type.2,y.type.2,sep="_")



general.name.x.first <- gsub( "\\|.*$", "", general.name.x )

general.name.x.second <- sub("^[^\\|]*", "", general.name.x)
general.name.x.second <- gsub("\\|","", general.name.x.second)


general.name.y.first <- gsub( "\\|.*$", "", general.name.y )

general.name.y.second <- sub("^[^\\|]*", "", general.name.y)
general.name.y.second <- gsub("\\|","", general.name.y.second)

lsa.out.3$y.type <- y.type
lsa.out.3$x.type.2 <- x.type.2
lsa.out.3$y.type.2 <- y.type.2
lsa.out.3$x.y.type <- x.y.type
lsa.out.3$x.y.type.2 <- x.y.type.2
lsa.out.3$general.name.x <- general.name.x
lsa.out.3$general.name.y <- general.name.y

#non binned plots of environmental data, with binned averages and temp an depth combined and then chl and tide combined
library(plotrix)

x <- rownames(subset(agg.data.w.tide, format(date.time,'%H') %in% c('10')))
y <- rownames(subset(agg.data.w.tide))

tide.12hr.esp.dates <- tide.12hr[c(354:11180),]

pdf("non.binned.env.parameters.esp.w.binned.points.pdf",useDingbats=F)
par(mfrow = c(3,1),
	oma = c(2,4,1,1) + 0.1,
         mar = c(1.5,1.1,4,3) + 0.1)
plot(env$date.time,env$Temperature,main="Temperature and Depth",type='l',ylim=c(12,17.5),col=adjustcolor("red",alpha.f = 0.2),las=2,xlab='',ylab='')

mtext("Temperature (C)",side=2,line=2.5)
for(a in aggdata.12hr[x,]$date.time) {
#15 and 4  hours previous, or something like that 
rect(xleft=a-54000, xright=a-14400, ybottom=-1, ytop=20,col=adjustcolor("grey85",alpha.f=0.5))
}
for(a in aggdata.12hr[y,]$date.time) {
#15 and 4  hours previous, or something like that 
rect(xleft=a, xright=a+1800, ybottom=17.5, ytop=18,col="black")
}
points(env$date.time,env$Temperature,main="Temperature",type='l',col=adjustcolor("red",alpha.f = 0.2))
points(agg.data.w.tide$date.time,agg.data.w.tide$Temperature,main="Temperature",col="red",type='b',cex=0.5,lwd=1.5)

par(new=T)

plot(env$date.time,env$Depth,type='l',col=adjustcolor("blue",alpha.f=0.2),axes=F,ylim=c(3.5,40))

points(env$date.time,env$Depth,main="Depth",type='l',col=adjustcolor("blue",alpha.f=0.2))

points(agg.data.w.tide$date.time,agg.data.w.tide$Depth,col="blue",type='b',cex=0.5,lwd=1.5)

axis(4,las=2)
mtext("Depth (m)",side=4,line=2.5)

#this parses the r output of *all* the taxa names...and also has the original med type id and taxon name
tail -n +2 prok.names.otu.name.conversion | cut -f2,3 -d ' ' > tail.prok.names.otu.name.conversion
tail -n +2 all.names.otu.name.conversion | cut -f2,3 -d ' ' > tail.all.names.otu.name.conversion
tail -n +2 chl.names.otu.name.conversion | cut -f2,3 -d ' '  > tail.chl.names.otu.name.conversion
tail -n +2 euk.names.otu.name.conversion | cut -f2,3 -d ' '  > tail.euk.names.otu.name.conversion

#
sort -k1,1 tail.prok.names.otu.name.conversion > sort.tail.prok.names.otu.name.conversion
sed -e 's/^/X/' silva.id.w.tax | sort -k1,1 > x.silva.id.w.tax
join -1 1 -2 1 x.silva.id.w.tax sort.tail.prok.names.otu.name.conversion > taxa.tag.plus.full.silva.tax

sed -e 's/^/X/' phytoref.id.w.tax | sort -k1,1 > x.phytoref.id.w.tax
join -1 1 -2 1 x.phytoref.id.w.tax sort.tail.chl.names.otu.name.conversion | sed 's/#/_/g' > taxa.tag.chl.plus.full.phytoref.tax

rev taxa.tag.chl.plus.full.phytoref.tax | cut -f1 -d ' ' | rev > taxa.names
sed 's/|/\t/g' taxa.tag.chl.plus.full.phytoref.tax > taxa.tag.chl.plus.full.phytoref.tax.2
paste taxa.names taxa.tag.chl.plus.full.phytoref.tax.2 > taxa.tag.chl.plus.full.phytoref.tax.3

sort -k1,1 pr2.id.w.tax > sort.pr2.id.w.tax
awk 'BEGIN { FS = OFS = "\t" } { for(i=1; i<=NF; i++) if($i ~ /^ *$/) $i = 0 }; 1' sort.pr2.id.w.tax > sort.pr2.id.w.tax.2

cat sort.pr2.id.w.tax | sed 's/^ /0 /g' | sed 's/  $/  0/g' | sed 's/  / 0/g' | head


sort -k1,1 tail.euk.names.otu.name.conversion > sort.tail.euk.names.otu.name.conversion
join -1 1 -2 1 sort.tail.euk.names.otu.name.conversion sort.pr2.id.w.tax.2 > taxa.tag.euk.plus.full.pr2.tax

require(heatmap3)
require(ape)
yellow.red <- colorRampPalette(c("lightyellow", "red","black"), space = "rgb")(100)

a16S.otu.tree <- read.tree("re.root.muscle.most.abundant.prok.heatmap.2.fasta.phylip_phyml_tree")

pdf("most.abundant.prok.tree.pdf")
plot(a16S.otu.tree)
dev.off()


a16S.otu.tree$edge.length[which(a16S.otu.tree$edge.length == 0)] <- 0.00001
a16S.otu.tree_um <- chronopl(a16S.otu.tree, lambda = 0.1, tol = 0)
a16S.otu.tree_d <- as.dendrogram(as.hclust.phylo(a16S.otu.tree_um))
a16S.otu.order <- order.dendrogram(a16S.otu.tree_d)
a16S.otu.tree_name <- labels(a16S.otu.tree_d)
a16S.otu.tree_position <- data.frame(a16S.otu.tree_name, a16S.otu.order)
a16S.otu.tree_position_2 <- a16S.otu.tree_position[order(a16S.otu.tree_position$a16S.otu.order),]



rna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select <- rna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select
dna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select <- dna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select
rna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select <- rna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select
dna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select <- dna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select


rownames(rna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select) <- rna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select[,1]
rownames(dna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select) <- dna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select[,1]
rownames(rna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select) <- rna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select[,1]
rownames(dna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select) <- dna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select[,1]

rna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.2 <- rna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select[,-1]
dna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.2 <- dna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select[,-1]
rna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.2 <- rna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select[,-1]
dna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.2 <- dna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select[,-1]

esp.dates.for.conversion.id.as.row.names <- read.table("esp.dates.for.conversion",sep="\t",row.names=1)

rna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.3 <- merge(rna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.2,esp.dates.for.conversion.id.as.row.names,by="row.names",all.y=T)
dna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.3 <- merge(dna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.2,esp.dates.for.conversion.id.as.row.names,by="row.names",all.y=T)
rna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.3 <- merge(rna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.2,esp.dates.for.conversion.id.as.row.names,by="row.names",all.y=T)
dna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.3 <- merge(dna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.2,esp.dates.for.conversion.id.as.row.names,by="row.names",all.y=T)

rna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.4 <- rna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.3[order(rna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.3$V2),][,-16]
dna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.4 <- dna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.3[order(rna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.3$V2),][,-16]
rna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.4 <- rna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.3[order(rna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.3$V2),][,-16]
dna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.4 <- dna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.3[order(dna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.3$V2),][,-16]

rownames(rna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.4) <- rna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.3[,1]
rownames(dna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.4) <- dna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.3[,1]
rownames(rna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.4) <- rna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.3[,1]
rownames(dna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.4) <- dna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.3[,1]

rna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.5 <- rna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.4[,-1]
dna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.5 <- dna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.4[,-1]
rna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.5 <- rna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.4[,-1]
dna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.5 <- dna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.4[,-1]


a16S.otu.matrix <- as.data.frame(cbind(t(rna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.5),a=NA,t(dna.dura.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.5),b=NA,t(rna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.5),c=NA,t(dna.ae.samples.prok.most.abundant.select.5)))

##write.table(a16S.otu.matrix,"prokaryote.heatmap.data.any.most.abundant.txt",quote=F,col.names=NA)

a16S.otu.tree_order <- match(a16S.otu.tree_position_2$a16S.otu.tree_name, row.names(a16S.otu.matrix))
a16S.otu.tree_ordered_matrix <- a16S.otu.matrix[a16S.otu.tree_order,]


pdf("most.abundant.ae.rna.prok.heatmap.pdf")
heatmap3(as.matrix(a16S.otu.tree_ordered_matrix),Rowv=a16S.otu.tree_d, Colv=NA, col = yellow.red, margins = c(3, 15),cexRow=0.75,cexCol=0.75, scale = 'none')


title("prok", line = -2, font=12)

dev.off()

a16S.otu.tree_d <- as.dendrogram(as.hclust.phylo(a16S.otu.tree_um))
a16S.otu.order <- order.dendrogram(a16S.otu.tree_d)
a16S.otu.tree_name <- labels(a16S.otu.tree_d)
a16S.otu.tree_position <- data.frame(a16S.otu.tree_name, a16S.otu.order)
a16S.otu.tree_position_2 <- a16S.otu.tree_position[order(a16S.otu.tree_position$a16S.otu.order),]

prok.dura.diff.ttest <- matrix(ncol=2, nrow=306)

for (i in c(2:306)) {

prok.dura.diff.ttest[i,1] <- t.test(prok.dura.diff[,i])$p.value
prok.dura.diff.ttest[i,2] <- t.test(prok.dura.diff[,i])$estimate
rownames(prok.dura.diff.ttest) <- colnames(prok.dura.diff)[1:306]

}

prok.dura.diff.ttest.subset <- subset(prok.dura.diff.ttest,prok.dura.diff.ttest[,1] > 0.05)

coef.variation <- function(x) {
    sqrt(var(x,na.rm=T))/mean(x,na.rm=T)
}

#rename columns
colnames(output.dna.dura.prok.cor.plus.peak.date) <- c("taxa","ratio","peak")

#compute mean for correlation values for each date
output.dna.dura.prok.cor.plus.peak.date.means <- aggregate(ratio ~ peak, output.dna.dura.prok.cor.plus.peak.date, FUN=mean)


#####IMPORT, CONVERT DATES
#import read dates and times to match those that are mm/dd/yyyy.#
esp.dates.for.conversion <- read.table("esp.dates.for.conversion",sep="\t")
colnames(esp.dates.for.conversion) <- c("peak","date")
#converted to dates
esp.dates.for.conversion$date <- as.POSIXct(as.character(esp.dates.for.conversion$date))
########

#merge mean correlation with real date
output.dna.dura.prok.cor.plus.peak.date.means.date <- merge(esp.dates.for.conversion,output.dna.dura.prok.cor.plus.peak.date.means,by="peak")
#merge all correlation with real date
output.dna.dura.prok.cor.plus.peak.date.date <- merge(output.dna.dura.prok.cor.plus.peak.date,esp.dates.for.conversion,by="peak")

#combine taxa names and lower and upper confidence intervals
order.subset.rna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates.w.confid <- as.data.frame(cbind(rownames(order.subset.rna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates)[1:126],as.data.frame(output.dna.rna.intercept.taxa),as.data.frame(output.dna.rna.intercept.taxa.2)))
colnames(order.subset.rna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates.w.confid) <- c("taxa","lower","upper")

#define whether or not the intercept confidence intervals are all above or below 0
order.subset.rna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates.w.confid$intercept <- ifelse(order.subset.rna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates.w.confid$upper > 0 & order.subset.rna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates.w.confid$lower > 0, "pos",ifelse(order.subset.rna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates.w.confid$upper < 0 & order.subset.rna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates.w.confid$lower < 0, "neg", "neither"))

#combine the table above with the one that has the ratios and the taxa names
output.dna.ae.prok.cor.plus.peak.date.date.w.intercept.merged <- merge(output.dna.ae.prok.cor.plus.peak.date.date,order.subset.rna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates.w.confid,by="taxa")


#plot
plot(output.dna.ae.prok.cor.plus.peak.date.date.w.intercept.merged$date,output.dna.ae.prok.cor.plus.peak.date.date.w.intercept.merged$ratio,ylab="Correlation Coefficient \n of RNA:DNA of Taxa",ylim=c(-0.25,1), col=ifelse(output.dna.ae.prok.cor.plus.peak.date.date.w.intercept.merged$intercept=="neg", "blue", ifelse(output.dna.ae.prok.cor.plus.peak.date.date.w.intercept.merged$intercept=="pos", 'red','black')))

numbers <- seq(1:53)
yellow.red <- colorRampPalette(c("lightyellow", "red","black"), space = "rgb")(75)
dend <- as.dendrogram(hclust(vegdist(rna.dura.samples.prok.w.dates,method="bray")))
labels_colors(dend) <-yellow.red[1:53]
plot(dend)


number.date <- as.data.frame(rbind(rownames(rna.dura.samples.prok.w.dates),seq(1:53)))
colnames(number.date) <- number.date[1,]


colLab <- function(n) {
    if (is.leaf(n)) {
        a <- attributes(n)
        labCol <- labelColors[numbers[which(names(numbers) == a$label)]]
        attr(n, "nodePar") <- c(a$nodePar, lab.col = labCol)
    }
    n
}

labels_colors(dend) <- yellow.red[numbers][order.dendrogram(dend)]

dna.dura.prok.colsums <- colMeans(for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok[,2:307])
rbind.dna.dura.prok.colsums <- t(rbind(for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok[,2:307],dna.dura.prok.colsums))

gr.0.rbind.dna.dura.prok.colsums <- t(subset(rbind.dna.dura.prok.colsums,rbind.dna.dura.prok.colsums[,41] > 0.001)[,-41])
cbind.for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok <- cbind(for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok[,1],gr.0.rbind.dna.dura.prok.colsums,for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok[,309:310])

rna.dura.prok.colsums <- colMeans(for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok[,2:307])
rbind.rna.dura.prok.colsums <- t(rbind(for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok[,2:307],rna.dura.prok.colsums))

gr.0.rbind.rna.dura.prok.colsums <- t(subset(rbind.rna.dura.prok.colsums,rbind.rna.dura.prok.colsums[,47] > 0.001)[,-47])
cbind.for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok <- cbind(for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok[,1],gr.0.rbind.rna.dura.prok.colsums,for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok[,309:310])

combined.colnames.table <- as.data.frame(table(c(colnames(cbind.for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok[1:184]),colnames(cbind.for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok[1:160]))))

taxa.that.are.above.threshold.both.datasets <- subset(combined.colnames.table,combined.colnames.table[,2] >1)[,1]

cbind.for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok <- cbind.for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok[(colnames(cbind.for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok) %in% taxa.that.are.above.threshold.both.datasets)]

cbind.for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok <- cbind.for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok[(colnames(cbind.for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok) %in% taxa.that.are.above.threshold.both.datasets)]

cbind.for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok <- cbind(cbind.for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok,for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok[,309:310])
cbind.for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok <- cbind(cbind.for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok,for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok[,309:310])

#make matrix size appropriate for generating day night p-values
output.day.night.dna.dura.prok <- matrix(ncol=2, nrow=131)

#output the correlation values rna to dna

for (i in c(2:131)) {

output.day.night.dna.dura.prok[i,1] <- t.test(cbind.for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok[,i] ~ cbind.for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok[,132],cbind.for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok,paired=T)$p.value
output.day.night.dna.dura.prok[i,2] <- t.test(cbind.for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok[,i] ~ cbind.for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok[,132],cbind.for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok,paired=T)$estimate
colnames(output.day.night.dna.dura.prok) <- c("p.value.dna.dura.prok","stat.dna.dura.prok")
rownames(output.day.night.dna.dura.prok) <- colnames(cbind.for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok)[1:131]
}

output.day.night.dna.dura.prok.w.mean <- cbind(output.day.night.dna.dura.prok,c(NA,colMeans(cbind.for.paired.t.test.dna.dura.prok[,2:131])))

#make matrix size appropriate for generating day night p-values
output.day.night.rna.dura.prok <- matrix(ncol=2, nrow=131)
#output the correlation values rna to rna
for (i in c(2:131)) {

output.day.night.rna.dura.prok[i,1] <- t.test(cbind.for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok[,i] ~ cbind.for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok[,132],cbind.for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok,paired=T)$p.value
output.day.night.rna.dura.prok[i,2] <- t.test(cbind.for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok[,i] ~ cbind.for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok[,132],cbind.for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok,paired=T)$estimate
colnames(output.day.night.rna.dura.prok) <- c("p.value.rna.dura.prok","stat.rna.dura.prok")
rownames(output.day.night.rna.dura.prok) <- colnames(cbind.for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok)[1:131]
}

output.day.night.rna.dura.prok.w.mean <- cbind(output.day.night.rna.dura.prok,c(NA,colMeans(cbind.for.paired.t.test.rna.dura.prok[,2:131])))

	oma = c(2,2,1,1) + 0.1,

x <- 1:30
for (i in x) {

plot(order.subset.dna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates[i,],order.subset.rna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates[i,],pch=20)
abline(lm(order.subset.rna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates[i,] ~ order.subset.dna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates[i,]))
title(rownames(order.subset.dna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates)[i],line=0.1,cex=0.3)
my.label <- round(mean(order.subset.rna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates[i,]/(order.subset.dna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates[i,]+0.00001),na.rm=T),2)
cor.label <- round(cor(order.subset.dna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates[i,], order.subset.rna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates[i,]),2)

text(x = max(order.subset.dna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates[i,])*0.9, y = max(order.subset.rna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates[i,])*0.9,labels=my.label,cex=1.2)
text(x = max(order.subset.dna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates[i,])*0.7, y = max(order.subset.rna.ae.samples.prok.w.dates[i,])*0.7,labels=cor.label,cex=1.2)

 }
